By contrasting intra-identity consistency against cross-identity impostors, it extracts identity-specific profiles that couple with query-conditioned adaptation via a parameter-free rule. This widens the separation between true and false matches while preserving score calibration. Evaluations on three clothes-changing person re-identification benchmarks show that our method consistently outperforms statistical, rank-based, and learned baselines, achieving up to an 8.8% absolute reduction in the false non-identification rate and demonstrating the value of identity-conditioned fusion in open-set person re-identification.

</details>


### [34] [SimCortex v2: Joint Cortical Surface Reconstruction with Near-Zero Collisions and Self-Intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07378)
*Kaveh Moradkhani,Sylvain Bouix*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SimCortex v2深度学习框架，可从T1加权MRI同时重建左右半球白质与软膜表面，在560例多队列数据上达到与最强基线相当的精度，同时显著减少网格自交与表面碰撞等几何伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 皮层薄且褶皱紧密，从结构MRI重建皮层白质（WM）与软膜（pial）表面仍是难题；现有方法会产生网格自交（self-intersection）与皮层表面间碰撞（collision）等几何伪影，深度学习虽将重建时间从数小时缩短至数分钟，但这些伪影依然存在。

Method: SimCortex v2：从体积分割估计拓扑正确的初始表面，并利用带状（ribbon）条件化的类U-Net网络预测多尺度平稳速度场（stationary velocity fields），对左右半球的WM与pial共四个表面进行联合细化。

Result: 在来自14个队列的560例（其中13个队列训练时未见）上评估，涵盖6–89岁、健康与临床人群及三家厂商扫描仪。SimCortex v2 与最强基线精度相当（平均对称表面距离0.253 mm），92.14%的病例未检测到表面间碰撞，自交比例在基于学习的方法中最低（0.044%）；而所有基线在每个病例中均至少产生一次碰撞。

Conclusion: SimCortex v2 在保持高表面距离精度的同时有效抑制几何伪影，具备跨年龄、跨人群与跨扫描仪的泛化能力；源代码、配置、预训练权重、预处理数据及精确评估划分均已公开。

Abstract: Reconstructing cortical WM and pial surfaces from structural magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) is a prerequisite for surface-based neuroanatomical analysis, yet remains challenging because the cortex is thin and tightly folded. Reconstruction methods can produce geometric artifacts such as mesh self-intersections and collisions between cortical surfaces, and although recent deep learning methods have reduced reconstruction time from hours to minutes, these artifacts persist. We propose SimCortex v2, a deep learning framework for simultaneous reconstruction of the left and right WM and pial surfaces from T1-weighted MRI. SimCortex v2 estimates topologically correct initial surfaces from a volumetric segmentation and refines all four jointly using multi-scale stationary velocity fields predicted by a ribbon-conditioned, U-Net-like network. We evaluated SimCortex v2 on 560 cases from 14 cohorts, thirteen of them unseen during training, spanning ages 6-89, healthy and clinical populations, and scanners from three vendors. SimCortex v2 matched the surface-distance accuracy of the strongest baseline (average symmetric surface distance 0.253 mm) while showing no detected inter-surface collision in 92.14% of cases and the lowest self-intersection fraction (0.044%) among learning-based methods, whereas every baseline produced at least one collision in every case. Source code, configuration files, pretrained weights, preprocessed data, and the exact evaluation splits are publicly released.

</details>


### [35] [GeoWM: Efficient Direct World Modeling in Explicit Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07381)
*Mehrdad Noori,Guile Wu,Sam Hosseini,Dongfeng Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GeoWM几何世界模型，利用几何基础模型构建几何历史，结合流匹配Transformer直接预测指定未来时刻的场景几何，配合相机运动预测器提升预测精度，在多个数据集上优于现有世界模型并显著降低推理时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶与机器人需要建模3D场景几何及其随时间的变化。现有世界模型范式通过预测未来图像或隐表示再恢复几何，未显式建模几何结构，且依赖递归展开(recursive rollout)以达到长时域预测，导致误差累积和计算成本增加。

Method: GeoWM基于几何基础模型将观测RGB帧转化为几何历史，用以条件化一个流匹配(flow-matching)Transformer，直接在指定未来时域预测场景几何，无需递归展开；同时引入轻量级相机运动预测器估计未来视角，并将观测几何投影到预测视角作为几何先验。

Result: 在覆盖城市驾驶、空中飞行和动态操作的四类数据集上，GeoWM在深度、相机位姿和3D场景几何预测方面均优于评估的世界模型，且在较长时间跨度下大幅降低推理时间。

Conclusion: 显式几何建模与直接时域预测可有效避免递归展开带来的误差累积与计算开销，未来视角投影作为几何先验能提升预测性能，为场景几何世界模型提供新范式。

Abstract: Modeling 3D scene geometry and its evolution over time is essential for autonomous driving and robotics. A common paradigm is to use world models to predict future images or latent representations of the environment and subsequently recover geometry from these predictions. However, this paradigm does not explicitly model geometric structure and typically relies on recursive rollouts to reach longer prediction horizons, leading to error accumulation and increasing computational cost. To address these limitations, we present GeoWM, a geometry world model that directly forecasts future scene geometry at specified future horizons without recursive rollout. The key idea is to leverage a geometry foundation model to transform observed RGB frames into a geometric history, which conditions a flow-matching transformer to predict the scene geometry at a specified future horizon. We further show that a lightweight camera-motion predictor can accurately estimate the future viewpoint, and that projecting the observed geometry into the predicted viewpoint provides an effective geometric prior for future geometry forecasting. Extensive experiments on four datasets spanning urban driving, aerial flight, and dynamic manipulation demonstrate that GeoWM outperforms the evaluated world models in forecasting depth, camera pose, and 3D scene geometry, while substantially reducing inference time at longer horizons.

</details>


### [36] [WildMatch: Weakly Supervised Image Matcher Adaptation for Wildlife Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07384)
*Turhan Can Kargin,Piotr Kubaty,Ekaterina Rostovskaya,Izabela Wierzbowska,Bartosz Zieliński,Marcin Przewięźlikowski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种弱监督适配方法，仅用身份标签对预训练关键点匹配器进行对比微调，用于个体动物重识别，在多个数据集上优于现成匹配器和局部-全局融合方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机陷阱影像中的个体动物重识别是非侵入式野生动物监测的核心任务。现有方法存在两难：一是将全局嵌入当作分类问题学习，需要每个个体大量标注图像且忽略局部特征证据；二是直接使用领域无关的现成图像匹配器，虽在大规模多样图像上预训练，但因野生动物数据集小而缺乏对应级标注，难以适配到该领域。

Method: 提出仅依赖身份标签的弱监督适配方案，无需关键点级或几何对应真值。流程为：用预训练匹配器挖掘信息量高的图像对；根据身份一致性构造弱正、负监督信号；对匹配网络进行对比微调，增强同身份图像对之间的对应关系、抑制不同身份图像对的对应关系。

Result: 在多个开源野生动物重识别数据集上，该方法在准确率上优于现成匹配器和一种最先进的局部-全局融合方法；在留出个体的开放世界协议下，模型学到的是可迁移的对应先验而非记忆训练身份。

Conclusion: 这是首个针对动物重识别进行匹配器级、身份监督适配的研究。该方法利用监测数据集中已有的身份标注，实现了图像匹配模型向野生动物领域的数据高效特化。

Abstract: Individual animal re-identification from camera-trap imagery is an instance retrieval problem central to non-invasive wildlife monitoring: a query image must retrieve the correct individual from a reference set of known animals. This requires computer vision models to recognize distinctive local patterns in fur, skin, or other visual markings. Current approaches either learn global embeddings as a classification problem, requiring many labeled images per individual while largely ignoring local evidence, or apply off-the-shelf, domain-agnostic image matchers. Although such matchers are pretrained on large and diverse image collections, adapting them to wildlife imagery is challenging because available datasets are small and lack correspondence-level annotations. We study weakly supervised adaptation of a pretrained keypoint matcher using only identity labels, without keypoint-level or geometric correspondence ground truth. We mine informative image pairs with the pretrained matcher, derive weak positive and negative supervision from identity agreement, and contrastively fine-tune the matching network to strengthen correspondences for same-identity pairs and suppress them for different identities. Across open-source wildlife re-identification datasets, our approach improves accuracy over off-the-shelf matchers and a state-of-the-art local--global fusion method. Under an open-world protocol with held-out individuals, it learns a transferable correspondence prior rather than memorizing training identities. To our knowledge, this is the first study of matcher-level, identity-supervised adaptation for animal re-identification. Our method enables data-efficient specialization of image matching models to wildlife domains using identity annotations already available in typical monitoring datasets.

</details>


### [37] [Semantic Capability Acquisition and Specialization During Vision-Language Model Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07385)
*Suguru Onda,Matthew Bailey,Ryan Farrell*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"语义能力轨迹"研究框架，追踪VLM微调过程中语义能力的获得、峰值与后续专业化衰退，并引入结构化语义路由(SSR)以探究监督形式对能力获得的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM微调评估通常只在单一的下游检查点进行，无法判断某项语义能力是"从未获得"还是"早期出现、后期在专业化过程中衰退"，因此需要从轨迹视角理解语义能力的获得、峰值、迁移与部署时的残留状态。

Method: 提出基于轨迹的框架，将能力获得、能力特定最优解、后期专业化三阶段分离；引入结构化语义路由(SSR)研究监督表示如何塑造所获得的能力；在DFN、MetaCLIP、OpenAI CLIP三类共六个预训练骨干上，围绕身份与属性两类能力进行训练全程追踪，并开展代表性诊断实验。

Result: 微调可获得超越预训练状态的语义能力，并在留出评估上观察到增益；非结构化的"名称+属性"监督能带来强名称-属性检索但无名称属性画像检索较弱，SSR显著提升无名称属性画像检索，且随机名称分支dropout可进一步增强；不同能力峰值阶段不同，按目标类名检索选出的检查点未必对应可迁移的语义最优。

Conclusion: 持续优化可能在保持强目标类名检索的同时削弱已获得的迁移性语义能力；诊断研究表明这种后期专业化与跨模态语义可及性下降一致，而图像单模态类别结构仍大量保留。说明VLM微调评估应转向轨迹视角。

Abstract: Fine-tuning vision-language models (VLMs) is typically evaluated at a single downstream checkpoint, obscuring whether a semantic capability was never acquired or emerged earlier and later declined during specialization. We ask how semantic capabilities are acquired, when they peak, how well they transfer, and what remains at deployment. We study these dynamics as a semantic capability trajectory, tracking identity- and attribute-based capabilities over training.
  We formulate a trajectory-based framework that separates capability acquisition, capability-specific optima, and later specialization, and introduce Structured Semantic Routing (SSR) to study how the representation of supervision shapes what is acquired. Across six pretrained backbones spanning DFN, MetaCLIP, and OpenAI CLIP, we show that fine-tuning can acquire semantic capability beyond the pretrained state, including gains observed on held-out evaluations. Unstructured name-and-attribute supervision produces strong name-and-attribute retrieval with comparatively weak name-free attribute-profile retrieval, whereas SSR yields substantially stronger name-free attribute-profile retrieval and is further strengthened by stochastic name-branch dropout. Different capabilities can peak at different stages, so a checkpoint selected by target class-name retrieval need not coincide with a transferable semantic optimum. Continued optimization can therefore preserve strong target class-name retrieval while reducing previously acquired transferable semantic capability. In a representative diagnostic study, this late specialization is consistent with reduced cross-modal semantic accessibility while substantial image-only class structure remains available.

</details>


### [38] [ElasticFit: Fit-Aware 3D Object Insertion via VLM Reasoning and Generative Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07460)
*Tzu-Hsin Hsieh,Ricardo Marroquim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ElasticFit 是一个由 VLM 引导的“适配感知”物体插入框架，通过将语言指令转化为显式 3D 拟合线索，实现刚性放置、均匀缩放与弹性变形三种自适应插入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向已有 3D 场景插入物体不只是选一个合理位置，还需让物体契合局部几何，同时保持语义意图与物理合理性。现有 VLM 与生成模型虽具备语义推理与视觉生成能力，但在物体必须嵌入受限局部空间时，缺乏 3D  grounding 与几何控制能力。因此需要一个能桥接高层语义推理与底层 3D 几何约束的插入框架。

Method: 提出 ElasticFit，一种以场景 grounded 表征为核心的 VLM 引导框架。输入为语言指令与渲染的场景观测，先由 VLM 推断结构化拟合线索，明确：接地位置、应占据的体积、朝向以及适配模式（刚性放置、均匀缩放、弹性拟合）。这些线索将高层 VLM 推理转化为显式 3D 约束，用于条件化物体生成并引导后续几何拟合。随后生成场景条件化的物体先验，重建为 3D，并通过模式特定的拟合细化网格，同时施加碰撞避免、接触一致性与物理接地约束。

Result: 在固定资产基线对比中，ElasticFit 将空间关系成功率从 50.8% 提升至 69.7%，支撑成功率从 48.3% 提升至 91.7%，均优于最强基线；并为复杂场景中的生成式“make-it-fit”插入提供了新能力。

Conclusion: ElasticFit 通过场景 grounded 的结构化拟合线索，把 VLM 的语义推理有效连接到 3D 几何生成与拟合，在空间关系与物理支撑等指标上显著超越基线，并能支持复杂场景下的生成式适配插入。

Abstract: Inserting objects into existing 3D scenes requires more than selecting a plausible location:
  the inserted object must also fit local geometry while preserving semantic intent and physical plausibility.
  Although recent Vision-Language Models (VLMs) and generative models enable semantic reasoning and visual content creation, they offer limited 3D grounding and geometric control when an inserted object must fit into constrained local spaces.
  We introduce \textbf{ElasticFit}, a VLM-guided framework for fit-aware object insertion centered on a novel scene-grounded representation.
  Given a language instruction and rendered scene observations, ElasticFit infers structured fitting cues that specify where the object should be grounded, what volume it should occupy, how it should be oriented, and its adaptation mode (rigid placement, uniform scaling, or elastic fitting).
  These cues convert high-level VLM reasoning into explicit 3D constraints that condition object generation and guide downstream geometric fitting.
  ElasticFit then generates a scene-conditioned object prior, reconstructs it in 3D, and refines the mesh through mode-specific fitting while enforcing collision avoidance, contact consistency, and physical grounding.
  In fixed-asset baseline comparisons, ElasticFit improves spatial relation success from 50.8\% to 69.7\% and support success from 48.3\% to 91.7\% over the strongest baseline, while providing novel support for generative "make-it-fit" insertions in complex scenarios.

</details>


### [39] [Protective Perturbations Must Survive the Resize: Scale-Robust Image Immunization against Malicious Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07464)
*Zhongliang Guo,Yan Lin,Yifei Qian,Daizong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文将图片缩放建模为频率选择性信道，揭示现有保护扰动在未知缩放因子下失效的原因，并提出尺度覆盖加权方法SRIM，在全分辨率照片上显著提升最坏情况下的抗编辑保护效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御性扰动（protective perturbation）在编辑器的固定工作分辨率下优化与评估，但实际分享照片多为千万像素级，编辑器会先以未知因子下采样。这一缩放环节使扰动衰减甚至失效，导致跨尺度保护能力不足，因此需要在未知缩放范围下实现最优的鲁棒保护。

Method: 将缩放建模为频率选择性信道，分析不同分辨率下计算的扰动的衰减与保护窗口；基于“最坏情况保护随尺度范围宽度仅对数下降、而按尺度取期望无法达到该界”的结论，提出SRIM：采样覆盖整个尺度范围的锚点尺度网格，并对当前最弱尺度赋予更高权重，以放弃标准期望为代价换取最坏情况保护。

Result: 在9–30百万像素全分辨率照片、2–8倍下采样条件下，SRIM将FLUX.2-klein编辑的最坏情况破坏度从最强已发表保护的0.192 LPIPS提升至0.463；在同等可见度下保护约翻倍。相同保护照片对9B模型与FLUX.2-dev的最坏情况分别为0.450和0.386（已发表保护至多0.184），并在InstructPix2Pix上也领先。

Conclusion: 将缩放视为频率选择性信道可为跨尺度保护提供理论指导；SRIM通过覆盖锚点尺度并对最弱尺度加权，在未知下采样因子下实现了显著更强的鲁棒保护，且可泛化到多个编辑模型。

Abstract: Protective perturbations aim to stop malicious instruction-guided editing of personal photos, but they are optimized and evaluated at the editor's working resolution, whereas shared photos have 10 megapixels or more and editors first downscale them by an unknown factor. We model this resize as a frequency-selective channel. In this model, a perturbation computed at the native resolution decays with the downscaling factor and is weak even without a resize, and a perturbation computed at a fixed working resolution protects only a window of scales. The best worst-case protection over an unknown range of scales degrades only logarithmically with the width of the range, and averaging over scales does not reach it. Guided by this analysis, we propose SRIM, which samples a grid of anchor scales covering the whole range, with weights that favor the currently weakest scale, at the cost of standard expectation over transformation. On full-resolution photos of 9 to 30 megapixels and downscaling factors from 2 to 8, SRIM raises the worst-case disruption of FLUX.2-klein edits from 0.192 LPIPS, attained by the strongest published protection, to 0.463. At equal visibility, it roughly doubles the protection. The same protected photos also protect against the 9B model and against FLUX.2-dev, with worst cases of 0.450 and 0.386 against at most 0.184 for published protections, and SRIM leads on InstructPix2Pix as well.

</details>


### [40] [LARK: A Low-Cost, Accurate, Occlusion-Resilient, Kalman Filter-Assisted Tracking System for Image-Guided Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07561)
*George Sideris,Justin Cree,Andrew Stirling,Mamadou Ly,Étienne Léger,D. Louis Collins*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LARK：用消费级RGB多相机与多视角冗余融合实现低成本光学跟踪，在遮挡下仍达亚毫米级精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商用立体红外跟踪器精度高但易受遮挡，且成本高昂，限制了图像引导手术（IGS）在资源有限场景中的普及与研究。

Method: 提出LARK多相机光学跟踪系统，采用消费级RGB硬件并结合多视角冗余与融合；开发并评估两种完整跟踪方法：多视角单目位姿融合（配自适应卡尔曼滤波）与多视角三角测量。

Result: 在精密加工栅格上，五相机下三角测量点定位中位目标配准误差0.64 mm，位姿融合轨迹跟踪0.73 mm；在解剖头模及不同遮挡水平下评估并与金标准立体红外系统对比；相机子集实验显示随可用视角减少精度平缓退化。

Conclusion: LARK以低于1000美元硬件成本提供亚毫米级精度的抗遮挡跟踪平台，适合IGS研究；硬件与软件及数据集已公开。

Abstract: Image-guided surgery (IGS) depends on accurate tracking of surgical instruments to provide real-time navigation relative to anatomical structures. Commercial stereo infrared trackers are accurate but prone to occlusion and cost-prohibitive for many settings. This work presents LARK, a multi-camera optical tracking system using commodity RGB hardware and multi-view redundancy and fusion. We develop and evaluate two complete tracking methods: multi-view monocular pose fusion and multi-view triangulation. Both methods are assessed under varying occlusion levels using a precision-machined grid and an anatomical head phantom, and compared against a gold-standard stereo infrared system. With five cameras and adaptive Kalman filtering, LARK achieves median target registration errors of 0.64 mm for point localization with triangulation and 0.73 mm for trajectory tracking with pose fusion on the machined grid. Camera-subset experiments show graceful degradation in adaptive pose-fusion accuracy as fewer views remain available. With tracking hardware costing under $1,000 USD, LARK provides a low-cost platform for image-guided surgery research. Hardware designs and software are publicly available at https://nist.mni.mcgill.ca/software/ , and datasets at https://nist.mni.mcgill.ca/data/ .

</details>


### [41] [AIMS: Anchor-Integrated Multi-View Synthesis for Scalable Novel View Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07566)
*JooHyun Park,HanYoung Jang,HyeongYeop Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AIMS框架通过将可用观测数量与全局合成模型处理的视图数量解耦，实现可扩展的前馈新视角合成。它用最远点采样选取固定数量的锚点视图，将邻近观测分组并融合为增强锚点表示，在RealEstate10K和ScanNet上取得优于Transformer和Gaussian基线的质量-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈新视角合成方法虽在已定位多视图输入上泛化能力强，但扩展到大规模视图集仍具挑战：基于Transformer的方法联合处理所有输入视图token，随视图数增长计算与内存急剧上升；而简单视图子采样又会丢弃潜在有用的观测信息。

Method: 提出Anchor-Integrated Multi-View Synthesis (AIMS)框架：使用最远点采样(farthest point sampling)选取固定数量、空间分布的锚点视图；将邻近观测围绕各锚点分组；采用轻量级可学习积分器(integrator)将其信息融合为增强的锚点表示。由此使额外观测也能参与合成，同时保持下游全局视图预算固定。

Result: 在RealEstate10K和ScanNet上评估，相对于Transformer和Gaussian基线展现出良好的质量-效率权衡。两数据集上分别达到29.41 dB和17.73 dB PSNR，渲染平均每视角7.24 ms。

Conclusion: AIMS通过解耦观测数量与全局处理视图数量，在保持固定全局视图预算的前提下有效利用额外观测，实现了可扩展且高效的前馈新视角合成。

Abstract: Feed-forward novel view synthesis methods achieve strong generalization from posed multi-view inputs, but scaling them to large input view sets remains challenging. Transformer-based approaches that jointly process all input-view tokens incur rapidly increasing computation and memory as the number of views grows, while simple view subsampling discards potentially useful observations. We introduce Anchor-Integrated Multi-View Synthesis (AIMS), a scalable framework that decouples the number of available observations from the number of views processed by the global synthesis model. AIMS selects a fixed set of spatially distributed anchor views using farthest point sampling, groups nearby observations around each anchor, and uses a lightweight learnable integrator to fuse their information into enriched anchor representations. This allows additional observations to contribute to synthesis while keeping the downstream global view budget fixed. Evaluations on RealEstate10K and ScanNet demonstrate a favorable quality--efficiency trade-off against transformer-based and Gaussian-based baselines. AIMS achieves 29.41 dB and 17.73 dB PSNR on the two datasets, respectively, with rendering averaging 7.24 ms per view.

</details>


### [42] [CETUS: How Far Do Representations Trained on Earth Transfer to Cassini SAR of Titan?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07576)
*Kevin Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CETUS 比较了 DINOv2、DOFA、CROMA 等预训练编码器特征与经典影像特征及未训练 ViT 特征在 Cassini SAR 影像土卫六地形分类中的跨域迁移表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用地球影像预训练表征服务行星地形分类，需要评估其从地球到土卫六的跨域迁移能力，并厘清特征来源、训练策略与分类器设置对结果的影响。

Method: 基于 USGS Cassini SAR 镶嵌影像，以专家地貌图地形标签为监督，在土卫六地理上分离区域进行预测；对比 DINOv2、DOFA、CROMA 与经典影像测量特征及未训练 ViT 特征，并在逻辑回归设置下考察特征缩放、优化与正则化的联合变化，以及进一步在土卫六数据上训练的影响。

Result: 逻辑回归设置下，预训练编码器平均宏 F1 高于组合经典特征；当特征缩放、优化与正则化同时变化时，编码器排名发生改变。继续用土卫六数据训练会提升 DINOv2、降低 DOFA，并对 CROMA 产生混合结果。

Conclusion: 架构与输入处理差异使该比较无法单独隔离预训练效应；评估行星制图表征迁移时需重视分类器拟合与各地形类别表现；由于地图部分依赖同一雷达观测，得分反映的是与专家解释的一致性。

Abstract: Cassini synthetic aperture radar (SAR) images reveal the dunes, plains, and lake basins of Titan, providing an instance of representations learned from Earth imagery for planetary terrain classification. Cross-domain Evaluation of Earth-to-Titan Transfer Using SAR (CETUS) compares features from DINOv2, DOFA and CROMA with classical image measurements and features from an untrained vision transformer on the U.S. Geological Survey's Cassini SAR mosaic. The classifiers learn terrain labels from an expert geomorphological map and predict those labels in geographically separate Titan regions. Under logistic regression settings, pretrained encoders achieve higher mean macro F1 than the combined classical features. Encoder rankings change when feature scaling, optimization, and regularization change together. Further training on Titan improves DINOv2 performance, degrades DOFA performance, and leads to mixed results for CROMA under the tested settings. Architectural and input processing differences prevent these comparisons from isolating the effect of pretraining. Classifier fitting and performance on individual terrain classes matter when assessing representation transfer for planetary mapping. Since the map draws partly on the same radar observations, the scores measure agreement with expert interpretation.

</details>


### [43] [REViT-v2: Hierarchical Windowed Roto-reflection Equivariant ViT for Equivariant Feature Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07585)
*Sheir A. Zaheer,Jihwan Moon,Chan Y. Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可扩展的旋转-反射群等变视觉Transformer（REViT-v2），基于窗口群卷积自注意力与分层特征架构，首次实现百万参数级别、ImageNet规模的群等变ViT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群等变视觉Transformer此前难以扩展到大规模参数与真实尺寸图像数据集，缺乏可扩展性，限制了其在ImageNet等标准基准上的应用。

Method: 采用窗口化群卷积自注意力（windowed group-convolutional self-attention）替代标准自注意力，并引入分层特征架构（hierarchical feature architecture），构建旋转-反射群（p4m）等变的视觉Transformer。

Result: REViT-v2可扩展至具有数百万参数的群等变ViT，并在ImageNet等实际尺寸图像的大规模数据集上训练，代码与预训练权重已开源。

Conclusion: 该工作将群等变视觉Transformer的可扩展性提升至ImageNet级别，为等变架构在大规模视觉任务中的实用化提供可行方案。

Abstract: We propose a scalable roto-reflection-group-equivariant vision transformer based on windowed group-convolutional self-attention and a hierarchical feature architecture. We demonstrate that our approach can be scaled to group-equivariant vision transformers (ViTs) with millions of parameters and large datasets with practically sized images, i.e., ImageNet. The code and pretrained weights for the proposed Hierarchical Windowed Roto-reflection Equivariant ViTs (REViT-v2) are available at https://github.com/kc-ml2/revit.

</details>


### [44] [PhysLDM: Latent Diffusion for High-Fidelity Deformable Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07609)
*Yu Zhang,Xudong Xu,Xingang Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysLDM：面向体素可变形物理的时空隐空间扩散范式，实现一次性高保真长时预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率三维体网格的长时程预测困难：自回归易累积误差，原生分辨率多帧直推计算代价过高，缺乏紧凑时空隐表示；且不确定确定性回归与生成式扩散哪种范式更合适。

Method: 提出统一隐扩散范式PhysLDM，核心为整体式时空VAE，避免常见视频VAE时间压缩的"阶梯"伪影；在可靠隐空间上系统对比回归与扩散，并采用隐扩散模型学习混沌形变分布。

Result: 在米级场景实现约2.48 mm重建精度、最高78倍token压缩；仅基于Objaverse规模数据运动学训练，单一模型零样本泛化至OOD数据集GSO与Toys4K；可微性支持逆问题求解与高阶设计优化。

Conclusion: PhysLDM为首个面向体素可变形动力学的高保真时空自编码器与隐扩散范式，提供了可扩展且鲁棒的神经仿真途径。

Abstract: Neural simulation of high-fidelity deformable bodies is a foundational challenge in computer graphics and physical AI. Long-horizon prediction for high-resolution 3D volumetric meshes is hard: autoregressive methods are susceptible to error accumulation, while direct multi-frame prediction at native resolution is computationally prohibitive. This motivates a compact spatiotemporal latent representation, which is largely unexplored for mesh-based volumetric physics. Meanwhile, it remains unclear whether deterministic regression or generative diffusion is the more appropriate predictive paradigm. To address these coupled challenges, we introduce PhysLDM, a unified latent-diffusion paradigm for one-shot volumetric deformable simulation. Its core is a holistic spatiotemporal VAE that avoids the "staircase" artifacts of standard temporal compression (as in common video VAEs), achieving ~2.48 mm reconstruction precision on meter-scale scenes at up to 78x token compression. Based on this reliable latent space, we systematically compare regression and diffusion methods. Our experiments uncover a key modeling insight: complex deformable dynamics are often chaotic, and in this regime deterministic regression tends to produce non-physical averages, whereas diffusion better models their distribution. Accordingly, we employ a latent diffusion model that effectively learns from the chaotic data to generate physically plausible trajectories. Trained purely kinematically on an Objaverse-scale dataset, a single PhysLDM generalizes zero-shot to unseen OOD datasets (GSO and Toys4K). Its differentiability further enables efficient solution of inverse problems and higher-order design optimization. To our knowledge, PhysLDM is the first high-fidelity spatiotemporal autoencoder and latent-diffusion paradigm for volumetric deformable dynamics, offering a scalable and robust approach to neural simulation.

</details>


### [45] [Disentangling Dual Image References in Frequency Aware Diffusion Models for Personalized Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07684)
*Haipeng Liu,Yang Wang,Meng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Dual-FDM，一种基于双参考（定制参考与颜色/风格参考）的频率感知扩散模型范式，通过频域掩码策略解耦不同频段，同时解决定制风格迁移与颜色风格迁移中的文本错位问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化图像生成中，现有扩散模型在图像定制时背景文本错位、风格迁移时前景文本错位。作者观察到该问题根源在于去噪过程中混合频段之间的纠缠。

Method: 提出Dual-FDM，在频域内用掩码策略解耦双图像参考的不同频段。定制风格迁移：用定制参考前景的中频段替换风格参考背景的中频段；颜色风格迁移：用颜色参考前景与背景的低频段替换风格参考背景的低频段。被替换的频段作为key和value，重建去噪图像的query前景与背景。

Result: 大量实验验证Dual-FDM在个性化图像生成上优于现有最先进扩散模型。

Conclusion: 通过频域解耦双参考的频段纠缠，Dual-FDM有效缓解文本错位，统一处理定制风格迁移与颜色风格迁移两类任务，代码已开源。

Abstract: Personalized image generation aims to synthesize text-driven images conditioned on reference images, while mainly casting the generation as image customization for foreground and style transfer for background. Previous arts of diffusion models suffers from the text misalignment with background for image customization and foreground for style transfer during the denoising process. Such facts, as we observed, rooted from the entanglement among hybrid frequency bands during the denoising process. To address such salient limitation, in this paper, we study personalized generation based on dual references - customization and color and style reference - and propose a paradigm to disentangle these Dual image references within Frequency-aware Diffusion Models, dubbed Dual-FDM, to simultaneously tackle two crucial personalized image generation tasks: customization style transfer and color style transfer, by disentangling different frequency bands via mask strategy within frequency domain. For customization style transfer, we replace the mid-frequency band of the background in the style reference with that from the foreground of the customized reference. For color style transfer, we substitute the low-frequency band of the background in the style reference with that from both the foreground and background of the color reference. Both the substituted frequency bands are used as the key and value to reconstruct the query foreground and background of the denoised personalized image.Extensive experiments validate the superiority of Dual-FDM over the state-of-the-art diffusion models for personalized image generation. Our code can be accessed from https://github.com/htyjers/Dual-FDM.

</details>


### [46] [Unlocking Fine-Grained Perception in CLIP via Structurally-Aware Latent Masked Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07689)
*Juntong Li,Lingwei Dang,Haomin Wu,Ziyan Qiu,Qingxin Xiao,Qingyao Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SALM是一个无需图文对的无监督嵌入对齐框架，通过结构感知的潜在掩码建模，融合局部几何先验与全局语义，提升CLIP的细粒度感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP等视觉-语言模型擅于全局语义对齐，但缺乏细粒度感知能力，制约了密集预测任务并成为多模态大模型的视觉瓶颈。现有引入视觉模型几何先验的方法难以同时实现局部空间结构与全局语义的深度对齐，甚至可能扭曲原始图文空间。

Method: 提出SALM框架，采用显式与隐式机制结合的双路径设计：1）双矩阵对齐策略，显式校准样本内空间相关性与激活强度，注入局部几何先验；2）潜在掩码建模机制，引导CLIP恢复目标模型缺失的语义细节，将细粒度结构隐式融入全局语义空间。基于CLIP浅层特征具有强空间观测能力的发现，进一步扩展为高效自蒸馏范式SALM-Self，无需外部模型即可释放CLIP内在细粒度潜力。

Result: 大量实验表明，SALM不仅显著提升密集预测任务性能，还提高了CLIP的零样本准确率，有效增强了多模态大模型的细粒度理解能力。

Conclusion: SALM通过无监督的结构感知潜在掩码建模实现局部与全局对齐的协同，并可通过自蒸馏范式高效扩展，为提升VLM细粒度感知提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) such as CLIP excel in global semantic alignment but often lack fine-grained perceptual capabilities. This hinders dense prediction tasks and bottlenecks the visual potential of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). Existing research has attempted to enhance CLIP's visual representations by incorporating geometric priors from vision-centric models. However, these strategies often struggle to achieve deep alignment for both local spatial structures and global semantics, potentially even distorting the original image-text space. To address these limitations, we propose SALM, an unsupervised embedding alignment framework based on structurally-aware latent mask modeling. SALM effectively synergizes local and global alignment via a dual-path design combining explicit and implicit mechanisms, without requiring any image-text pairs. First, we introduce a dual-matrix alignment strategy that explicitly calibrates intra-sample spatial correlations and activation intensities, thereby effectively injecting local geometric priors. Based on this, we further design a latent mask modeling mechanism to guide CLIP to restore the missing semantic details of the target model, thereby implicitly aggregating fine-grained structures into the global semantic space. Furthermore, driven by the empirical observations that CLIP's shallow features inherently possess strong spatial observational capabilities, we naturally extend SALM to a highly efficient self-distillation paradigm, SALM-Self. This unlocks CLIP's intrinsic fine-grained potential without relying on any external models. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SALM not only significantly improves performance in dense prediction tasks but also boosts CLIP's zero-shot accuracy, effectively enhancing the fine-grained understanding capabilities of MLLMs. Project page at https://qzfm.github.io/salm_project_page/.

</details>


### [47] [Anchor-driven Multi-modal Multi-scale Expert Selection for Survival Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07694)
*Tao Zhou,Ying Hu,Huazhu Fu,Yi Zhou,Xiao-Jun Wu,Haibin Ling*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AM²ES框架，通过锚点驱动的多模态对齐与层次化专家选择，实现病理WSI与转录组整合的生存预测，在TCGA多队列达到SOTA并具备细粒度可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WSI与转录组整合方法存在两处不足：一是将多模态特征直接投影到共享隐空间而缺乏显式对齐，导致形态线索与分子信号错配纠缠；二是融合策略对WSI极端空间异质性等同处理，缺少按患者自适应优先选择临床相关组织尺度的机制。

Method: 提出AM²ES框架（Anchor-driven Multi-modal Multi-scale Expert Selection）。其一为锚点驱动多模态融合（AMF）模块，引入可学习语义锚点作为跨模态中介，通过对转录组特征与多尺度病理表示施加结构正则化对齐来弥合语义鸿沟；其二为在对齐语义空间上构建的层次化专家混合（H-MoE）选择模块，将预后选择解耦为两个层次：尺度内专家过滤（Intra-scale Expert Filtering），在各放大倍数下判别性识别显著肿瘤区域；以及跨尺度层次路由（Inter-scale Hierarchy Routing），动态加权并选择最具信息量的分辨率层级。

Result: 在多个TCGA癌症队列上的大量实验表明，AM²ES取得状态最优性能，并可通过可视化特定分子通路如何驱动跨组织尺度的专家路由决策，提供细粒度可解释性。代码将于https://github.com/taozh2017/AM2ES 发布。

Conclusion: AM²ES以语义锚点对齐缓解跨模态语义鸿沟，并以层次化专家选择模拟病理学家诊断流程自适应处理空间异质性，在提升多模态生存预测精度的同时实现分子通路与组织尺度间决策路径的可解释性。

Abstract: The integrative analysis of histopathological Whole-Slide Images (WSIs) and transcriptomic profiles holds significant promise for cancer survival prediction. However, existing methods typically project multi-modal features directly into a shared latent space without explicit alignment, leading to the entanglement of mismatched morphological cues and molecular signals. Furthermore, current fusion strategies often treat the extreme spatial heterogeneity of WSIs uniformly, lacking mechanisms to adaptively prioritize clinically relevant tissue scales for individual patients. To address these limitations, we propose an Anchor-driven Multi-modal Multi-scale Expert Selection (AM$^2$ES) framework for survival prediction. Specifically, we present an Anchor-driven Multi-modal Fusion (AMF) module, which introduces learnable semantic anchors as cross-modal mediators to bridge the semantic gap by enforcing a structurally regularized alignment between transcriptomic features and multi-scale pathology representations. Built upon this aligned semantic space, we further design a Hierarchical Mixture-of-Experts (H-MoE) selection module to decouple the hierarchical prognostic selection process. Mimicking the pathologist's diagnostic workflow, H-MoE performs (i) Intra-scale Expert Filtering to discriminatively identify salient tumor regions within each magnification, and (ii) Inter-scale Hierarchy Routing to dynamically weight and select the most informative resolution levels. Extensive experiments on multiple TCGA cancer cohorts demonstrate that our AM$^2$ES achieves state-of-the-art performance while offering fine-grained interpretability by visualizing how specific molecular pathways drive the expert routing decisions across tissue scales. The code will be released at https://github.com/taozh2017/AM2ES.

</details>


### [48] [RBMatch: Dual-Level Class Rebalancing for Semi-Supervised Building Footprint Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07698)
*Akil Ahmad Taki,Shaikh Anowarul Fattah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 RBMatch 双层级类别再平衡半监督框架，同时调控伪标签生成与无监督优化，解决遥感建筑提取中的前景-背景不平衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率遥感影像的建筑足迹提取对城市规划、灾害响应和环境监测至关重要，但稠密像素级标注成本高昂，因此采用半监督学习利用无标注影像。遥感场景中严重的前景-背景不平衡给自训练带来挑战：会偏置伪标签生成，并使无监督优化偏向多数背景类。作者指出仅在单一阶段处理不平衡并不足够——仅平衡伪标签选择无法阻止背景偏置在无监督损失优化阶段重新出现，这一失效模式被命名为"imbalance leak"（不平衡泄漏）。

Method: RBMatch 是一个双层级类别再平衡框架，联合调控伪标签生成与无监督优化。它结合一条监督学习路径与一个自训练模块，后者包含三个组件：自适应类别特定阈值（ACT）用于平衡的伪标签选择；置信度感知的类别平衡重加权（CACBR）用于缓解无监督损失中的类别偏置；分布对齐（DAL）用于使无标注数据的预测分布匹配有标注数据的先验分布。

Result: 在 WHU、INRIA 和 Massachusetts 三个建筑足迹数据集上，于 1%–10% 标注比例下进行实验，RBMatch 在所评估方法中一致取得最佳的建筑 IoU 和 F1 分数。改进在高度不平衡的 Massachusetts 数据集上最为显著：在 1% 标注比例下，IoU 比最强基线提升 1.37 个百分点，并且是唯一在全部十二个数据集-比例设置下均超越全监督基线的方法。

Conclusion: RBMatch 通过双层级类别再平衡有效缓解了自训练中的不平衡泄漏问题，在多个遥感建筑足迹数据集和低标注比例下稳定优于现有半监督方法及全监督基线，验证了同时调控伪标签生成与无监督优化的必要性。

Abstract: Accurate building footprint extraction from high-resolution remote sensing imagery is essential for urban planning, disaster response, and environmental monitoring. However, obtaining dense pixel-level annotations is costly, motivating the use of semi-supervised learning (SSL) to leverage unlabeled imagery. In remote sensing, severe foreground--background imbalance poses a particular challenge for self-training, as it can bias pseudo-label generation and the resulting unsupervised optimization toward the majority background class. We show that addressing this imbalance at only one stage is insufficient: balancing pseudo-label selection alone does not prevent background bias from re-emerging during unsupervised loss optimization, a failure mode we term \emph{imbalance leak}. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{RBMatch}, a dual-level class-rebalancing framework that jointly regulates pseudo-label generation and unsupervised optimization. RBMatch combines a supervised learning pathway with a self-training module comprising three components: adaptive class-specific thresholding (ACT) for balanced pseudo-label selection, confidence-aware class-balanced reweighting (CACBR) for mitigating class bias in the unsupervised loss, and distribution alignment (DAL) for matching the predicted unlabeled-data distribution to the labeled-data prior. Experiments on the WHU, INRIA, and Massachusetts building footprint datasets across labeled ratios of 1%--10% show that RBMatch consistently achieves the best building IoU and F1-score among the evaluated methods. The improvement is most pronounced on the highly imbalanced Massachusetts dataset, where RBMatch improves IoU by 1.37 points over the strongest baseline at a 1% labeling ratio and is the only method to outperform the fully supervised baseline across all twelve dataset--ratio settings.

</details>


### [49] [What Frame-Level Labels Can and Cannot Do for Small-UAV Point Detection in Thermal Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07705)
*Wonbin Son,Gyumum Choi,Junil Seo,Hyungjoon Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究仅使用帧级“存在/缺失”标签、无需空间标注的无人机点检测架构，在冻结分类学到的空间特征后训练读出层生成空间得分图与点检测，并在两个红外数据集上评估定位命中率与虚警约束下的检测率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UAV使用增长使图像检测需求上升，但基于学习的检测器依赖标注，传感器或场景变化时空间标注成本高。作者关注在只有帧级存在/缺失标签时学习定位，减轻额外标注负担。

Method: 采用既有小UAV点检测架构：冻结通过分类学习到的空间特征，用相同帧级标签训练读出层，输出空间得分图与点检测，不依赖外部检测器。在两个热红外数据集CST Anti-UAV与Anti-UAV410上评估命中率与虚警约束下检测率，分析训练阶段、标签分配、合成与模型配置，并与边界框检测器比较；还探索AOT可见光图像与帧标签应用。

Result: 分类训练增强目标相关空间响应，读出训练帮助一致提取；将相近标签数量分散到更多视频可提高定位命中率，合成效应因数据集与评价标准而异；更高定位命中率不一定改善虚警约束下的检测，目标信号弱于背景变化及跨数据集迁移时仍会失败。

Conclusion: 研究为标签分配、空间表征与读出、合成以及虚警控制提供指导，表明帧级标签可在无空间标注下支持小UAV点检测，但受目标信号强度、背景变化与跨域迁移限制。

Abstract: The growing use of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) has increased the importance of image-based UAV detection. Learning-based detectors are trained on imagery and annotations, with annotation type determining the information available during training. We focus on learning localization from frame-level target presence/absence labels when sensor or scene changes make spatial annotations for additional training burdensome. We analyze the detection capability, learning behavior, and potential applications of an existing architecture for point detection of small UAVs, trained with presence/absence labels and requiring no external detector. The architecture freezes spatial features learned through classification and trains a readout with the same frame labels to produce spatial score maps and point detections. On two thermal infrared datasets, CST Anti-UAV and Anti-UAV410, we evaluate localization hit rates and detection rates under false-alarm constraints, analyze the effects of training stages, label allocation, synthesis, and model configuration, and compare with bounding-box detectors. We also explore potential applications on Airborne Object Tracking (AOT) using its visible-light imagery and frame labels. Classification training strengthened target-related spatial responses, while readout training helped extract them consistently. Distributing similar label counts across more videos yielded higher localization hit rates, while synthesis effects varied by dataset and evaluation criterion. Higher localization hit rates did not always improve detection under false-alarm constraints, and failures remained when target signals were weak relative to background variation and under cross-dataset transfer. These findings provide guidance on label allocation, spatial representations and readouts, synthesis, and false-alarm control.

</details>


### [50] [Comprehensive Evaluation and Fine-Tuning of Foundational Cell Nuclei Segmentation Models in Renal Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07711)
*Ruijie Wu,Junlin Guo,Ruining Deng,Yu Wang,Shilin Zhao,Haichun Yang,Yuankai Huo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过整合Easy伪标签、Medium新标注与Hard共识失败病例，构建多难度级别人工参与框架，系统评估7种微调策略与9种细胞分割模型，验证多难度标注对肾脏病理细胞核实例分割模型适配的有效性，但最优标注组成具有模型依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肾脏病理定量分析依赖精准的细胞核实例分割，但通用模型在低对比度、细胞核密集、形态复杂及背景染色强的场景下表现不佳，且缺乏跨分割难度的标注数据与系统性微调策略评估。

Method: 扩展人工参与（human-in-the-loop）框架，整合三类不同难度标注：5,901个由基础模型在易分割病例上生成的伪标签（Easy）、860个新专家标注的未解决疑难病例（Medium）、198个专家标注的共识失败病例（Hard）。基于这些标注，对9种细胞分割模型配置开展7种单源与混合源微调策略的系统评估。

Result: 微调提升了所有模型性能，Medium数据出现在9种最佳策略中的7种。LSP-DETR在仅用Hard数据微调时取得最高F1分数0.8725；StarDist提升幅度最大，仅用Medium数据微调后F1从0.7380提升至0.8332。

Conclusion: 跨多个难度级别的标注能够有效支持模型适配，但最优标注组成因模型而异，需针对不同模型定制标注策略。

Abstract: Accurate nuclei instance segmentation is essential for quantitative renal pathology, yet general-purpose models often struggle with low contrast, dense nuclei, complex morphology, and strong background staining. In this work, we extended a human-in-the-loop framework by combining 5,901 foundation-model-generated pseudo-labels from well-segmented cases (Easy), 860 newly expert-annotated unresolved challenging cases (Medium), and 198 expert-annotated consensus failure cases (Hard). These annotations, spanning different levels of segmentation difficulty, enabled the systematic evaluation of seven single-source and mixed-source fine-tuning strategies across nine cell segmentation model configurations. Fine-tuning improved all models, with Medium data included in seven of the nine best-performing strategies. LSP-DETR achieved the highest F1 score of 0.8725 with Hard-only fine-tuning, while StarDist showed the largest improvement, increasing from 0.7380 to 0.8332 with Medium-only fine-tuning. These findings show that annotations spanning multiple difficulty levels support effective model adaptation, although the optimal annotation composition remains model dependent.

</details>


### [51] [RefRoute: Decoupling Conditioning Cost from References via Compact Residual Conditioning and Spatial Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07720)
*Wanning He,Yuyao Zhang,Yu-Wing Tai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RefRoute 通过紧凑残差条件表示与条件/注意力路由机制，降低多参考图生成中参考 token 与注意力开销，并在多参考场景实现显著性能与推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多参考图像生成方法通常将参考图编码为密集视觉 token 网格并用全局注意力联合处理，随着参考图数量与分辨率增加，条件表示与注意力计算成本急剧上升，难以扩展到“多参考”场景。

Method: 提出 RefRoute 框架，包含两个互补机制：1）紧凑残差条件（Compact residual conditioning）：用低分辨率 latent token 结合从全分辨率像素提取的轻量残差特征，在减少参考 token 数量的同时保留细粒度外观线索；2）条件路由与注意力路由（Condition routing and attention routing）：将参考 token 与其分配的目标区域对齐，限制跨参考交互，同时允许在区域边界之外进行选择性参考访问以整合场景。此外构建 RefRoute-Data 用于训练多参考生成模型，并发布 ManyRef100 基准，覆盖人物、物体及混合组合，含 10–17 张参考图。

Result: 经多参考微调后，RefRoute 在 ManyRef100 上取得总体 Weighted-Ref-VIEScore 36.06，显著高于 FLUX.2-Klein-9B 的 8.88。独立推理成本评估显示，随参考数量增加延迟增长明显更慢：在 16 张参考下，50 步与 4 步配置分别相比对应 FLUX 基线实现 18.3× 与 14.2× 加速。

Conclusion: 结果表明，紧凑参考表示与空间路由注意力是实现可扩展多参考图像生成的有效途径。

Abstract: Multi-reference image generation requires preserving the appearance of multiple subjects while composing them into a coherent scene. However, existing diffusion transformers commonly encode references as dense visual token grids and jointly process them with global attention, making conditioning increasingly expensive as the number and resolution of references grow. We present RefRoute, a framework that addresses both reference representation cost and attention overhead through two complementary mechanisms. Compact residual conditioning combines low-resolution latent tokens with lightweight residual features extracted from full-resolution pixels, reducing reference token counts while retaining fine-grained appearance cues. Condition routing and attention routing align reference tokens with their assigned target regions and restrict cross-reference interactions, while allowing selective reference access beyond region boundaries for scene integration. We further introduce RefRoute-Data for training many-reference generation models and ManyRef100, a benchmark spanning human, object, and mixed compositions with 10-17 references. After many-reference fine-tuning, RefRoute achieves an overall Weighted-Ref-VIEScore of 36.06 on ManyRef100, compared with 8.88 for FLUX.2-Klein-9B. Separate inference-cost evaluations show substantially slower latency growth as the reference count increases: at 16 references, our 50-step and 4-step configurations achieve $18.3\times$ and $14.2\times$ speedups over their corresponding FLUX baselines, respectively. These results establish compact reference representations and spatially routed attention as an effective approach to scalable many-reference image generation.

</details>


### [52] [Structure-aware Keypoint Localization for Videofluoroscopic Swallowing Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07726)
*Kai Zhou,Chuanshen Chen,Runhao Zeng,Meng Dai,Yifan Yang,Jinwu Hu,Daiyuan Li,Mingkui Tan,Fei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对VFSS吞咽造影中解剖关键点定位问题，提出扩展标注至软腭并纳入大规模无标注数据的VFSSKep数据集，以及克服空间偏置的结构感知半监督关键点定位框架S³KL，在半监督设定下取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VFSS是吞咽障碍诊断的金标准之一，其自动化运动学分析依赖精确的解剖关键点定位。然而现有研究存在三方面不足：(1) 关键点覆盖有限，多聚焦颈椎或舌骨，忽略软腭等关键区域；(2) 仅标注主动吞咽片段，忽略大量非吞咽数据，数据效率低；(3) 直接使用通用半监督方法利用无标注数据效果不佳，因医学X射线布局固定，模型易记忆绝对坐标而非理解解剖结构，产生空间偏置。

Method: 提出VFSSKep数据集，扩展标注至软腭并纳入大规模无标注数据；提出S³KL结构感知半监督关键点定位框架，包含两项策略：(1) Structure-Aware Learning，提取高分辨率结构线索以进行结构感知表征学习；(2) Structural Representation Consistency Learning，结合块洗牌(block shuffling)以强制不变的结构识别。

Result: 实验表明该方法取得当前最优的半监督性能，即使在仅使用无标注数据与25%标注数据的条件下，也超过了使用100%标注数据的全监督学习。

Conclusion: 通过扩展关键点与数据标注范围、并引入结构感知的半监督学习策略，可有效缓解医学X射线固定布局导致的空间偏置问题，显著提升VFSS关键点定位的数据效率与精度，代码与数据将公开于 https://github.com/kaai520/S3KL。

Abstract: Videofluoroscopic Swallowing Study (VFSS) is one of the gold standard for diagnosing swallowing disorders, providing dynamic X-ray imaging of the swallowing process. Automated kinematic analysis in VFSS relies fundamentally on precise anatomical keypoint localization. However, existing studies focus on limited keypoints (e.g., cervical vertebrae or the hyoid) and overlook critical regions such as the soft palate, while annotating only active swallowing segments and ignoring abundant non-swallowing data, resulting in poor data efficiency. Moreover, leveraging this unlabeled data via standard semi-supervised learning is suboptimal, as generic methods are prone to spatial bias. In medical X-rays with fixed layouts, models tend to memorize absolute coordinates rather than understanding anatomical structures. To tackle these challenges, we introduce VFSSKep, a novel dataset that extends annotations to the soft palate and incorporates large-scale unlabeled data. We further propose S$^3$KL, a Structure-aware Semi-Supervised Keypoint Localization framework designed to overcome spatial bias. It integrates a Structure-Aware Learning strategy to extract high-resolution structural cues for structure-aware representation learning, and a Structural Representation Consistency Learning strategy with block shuffling to enforce invariant structural recognition. Experiments show our method achieves state-of-the-art semi-supervised performance, even with unlabeled and 25% labeled data surpassing fully supervised learning with 100% labeled data. Code and data will be made publicly available at: https://github.com/kaai520/S3KL.

</details>


### [53] [Foveated Compression: Selective High-Resolution Preservation for Token-Efficient VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07729)
*Donghyun Han,Jangho Park,Yuseok Bae*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Foveated Compression：将全分辨率图像编码一次，用原生分辨率与压缩分辨率视觉token的混合表示图像，在固定token预算下探讨视觉保真度应在何处保留。行为自蒸馏的Foveated Merger负责局部压缩并保持与原生token兼容，轻量Foveated Selector从九个空间单元中选一个保留原生分辨率。结果显示局部保真并非普遍更优，区域选择与压缩区域保真度存在互补瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉token是视觉语言模型推理成本的主要来源，而简单的图像下采样仍是出人意料的强压缩基线。由此引出核心问题：在固定token预算下，视觉保真度应当保留在何处？现有均匀下采样无法回答该空间分配问题。

Method: 提出Foveated Compression：全分辨率图像仅编码一次，用原生分辨率与压缩分辨率token混合表示。行为自蒸馏的Foveated Merger压缩局部视觉token，同时保持与原生token的兼容性；轻量Foveated Selector通过穷举、预算匹配的干预监督，从九个空间单元中选择一个以原生分辨率保留。

Result: 在11.11%视觉token下，均匀Foveated Compression与等token下采样无显著配对差异。在20.99%下，学习到的选择器显著优于随机与固定分配，但低于强整图缩放。预算匹配的区域选择oracle达82.73宏观准确率，学习选择器仅69.61，显示同一空间动作空间内仍有大幅提升空间。匹配探针表明，预测压缩何时破坏答案的信号在语言模型计算后更易获得。

Conclusion: 局部保真并非普遍优于整图缩放；区域选择与压缩区域保真度两方面存在互补瓶颈。轻量预填充无关选择器难以获取判断压缩何时破坏答案的信号，而语言模型计算后信号更易得，指向改进选择机制的潜力。

Abstract: Visual tokens are a major source of inference cost in vision-language models, yet simple image downsampling remains a surprisingly strong compression baseline. This raises a complementary question: under a fixed token budget, where should visual fidelity be preserved? We introduce Foveated Compression, which encodes a full-resolution image once and represents it with a mixture of native- and compressed-resolution visual tokens. A behaviorally self-distilled Foveated Merger compresses local visual tokens while preserving compatibility with their native counterparts, and a lightweight Foveated Selector chooses one of nine spatial cells to retain at native resolution using exhaustive budget-matched intervention supervision. At 11.11% visual tokens, uniform Foveated Compression shows no significant paired difference from iso-token downsampling. At 20.99%, the learned selector significantly outperforms random and fixed allocation, but remains below strong whole-image resizing, showing that localized fidelity is not universally preferable. A budget-matched region-choice oracle reaches 82.73 macro accuracy versus 69.61 for the learned selector, revealing substantial headroom within the same spatial action space. Matched probing further shows that signals predicting when compression breaks the answer are substantially more accessible after language-model computation than to the lightweight prefill-free selector. These results expose complementary bottlenecks in region selection and compressed-region fidelity.

</details>


### [54] [Later Is Better: Token Reduction for ViTs Under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07758)
*Hyeongheon Cha,Hyungjun Yoon,Sung-Ju Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种单参数"后段集中幂律"token削减调度，在不增加推理成本的情况下显著提升视觉Transformer在分布偏移下的精度保持率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免训练token削减方法主要在干净数据上设计与评估，在真实分布偏移下其相对未压缩模型的精度差距会随削减率增大而扩大。作者发现该差距由削减调度（即削减的深度分布）主导，而这一调度通常被固定为实现细节，未被研究。

Method: 引入单参数的"后段集中幂律"（late-concentrated power-law）调度，替代传统的均匀削减调度，无需额外推理成本、无需逐输入或逐领域调参；通过单层探针分析机制。

Result: 在ImageNet-C与DeiT-S上，后段调度在26%计算削减下弥补83%的精度差距（+1.17pp），在7%削减下弥补99%（+0.26pp）；在与均匀调度等计算量约束下仍胜出（总削减更多、末尾保留更少）。结论在5种token削减方法（ToMe、EViT、ATS、ATC、PiToMe）、9种骨干、所有ImageNet-C损坏类型、8个额外偏移套件及视频与视觉语言问答两种模态上成立；干净数据上仍为正收益，并随严重度单调上升至severity 5时约4倍；在6种测试时适应方法下仍保持增益。

Conclusion: 削减的深度调度是决定token削减在分布偏移下鲁棒性的关键因素；后段集中幂律调度普遍有效且增益对偏移具有特异性，机制在于早期削减会使特征经过更多剩余层从而将削减误差前置化。

Abstract: Training-free token reduction accelerates vision transformers by removing redundant tokens across layers, recovering most of the original accuracy at a fraction of the compute. These methods, however, are designed and evaluated primarily on clean data, and under real-world distribution shift their accuracy gap to the uncompressed model widens with the removal rate. We show that this gap is governed by the reduction schedule, the depth profile of removal, usually left fixed as an implementation detail. Concretely, we introduce a one-parameter late-concentrated power-law schedule that consistently improves out-of-distribution accuracy over flat at no extra inference cost. On ImageNet-C with DeiT-S, the late schedule closes 83% of that gap at a 26% compute reduction (+1.17pp), and 99% of it at a lighter 7% reduction (+0.26pp). The gain cannot be attributed to retaining more tokens or using extra compute: held to flat's compute, the late schedule removes more tokens in total and leaves fewer tokens at the end, yet still wins. Single-layer probes point to a mechanism: earlier reductions perturb features that pass through more remaining layers, front-loading reduction error in depth. The effect is broad, holding across five token-reduction methods (ToMe, EViT, ATS, ATC, PiToMe), nine backbones, all ImageNet-C corruption types, eight further shift suites, and two further modalities, video and vision-language QA. It is also specific to shift, still positive on clean and rising monotonically to ~4x that at the highest severity 5. The schedule keeps its gain under six test-time adaptation methods, and needs no per-input or per-domain tuning.

</details>


### [55] [From Laboratory to Road: Evaluating Wearable Gaze Accuracy for Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07783)
*William Engel,Fabian Flohr*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 首个统一的穿戴式视线追踪精度量化框架，用于真实驾驶条件下评估BEV表征的行为信号可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BEV表征已成为自动驾驶感知与规划的常用接口，但仅编码场景内容而非对驾驶行为相关的信息；可穿戴眼动仪常被当作空间真值使用，却对头部运动、光照与标定漂移敏感。

Method: 开展实车道路研究，纳入41个有效场景，由一名驾驶员注视车辆车牌；以车牌中心与眼镜估计视线方向间的角差衡量视线误差；另设室内对照实验，系统分析距离、光照、头部运动、目标运动与视线偏心率对系统偏差和精度的影响。

Result: 实车平均误差4.58度；应用由室内记录估计的偏移量后降至1.10度，且41个场景全部改善；该偏移量在会话间存在差异。

Conclusion: 可靠的BEV监督可能需要在线重标定及依赖条件的视线不确定性估计。

Abstract: Bird's-eye-view (BEV) representations have become a widely used interface between perception and planning in autonomous driving, but they encode what is in a scene, not what is behaviorally relevant to a human driver. Gaze offers a compelling behavioral signal for this gap, yet wearable eye trackers are routinely deployed as if their spatial output were ground truth, despite known sensitivity to head motion, illumination, and calibration drift. We present, to our knowledge, the first unified framework for quantifying wearable gaze accuracy under real driving conditions. Our on-road study contains 41 validated scenes in which one driver fixated a vehicle's license plate. Gaze error is measured as the angular difference between the plate center and the gaze direction estimated by the glasses. Separate indoor studies with the same driver and device systematically analyze how distance, illumination, head motion, target motion, and gaze eccentricity affect both systematic bias and gaze precision. The mean on-road error was 4.58 degrees. Applying an offset estimated from the indoor recordings reduced it to 1.10 degrees and improved all 41 scenes. Because this offset varied between sessions, reliable BEV supervision may require online recalibration and condition-dependent estimates of gaze uncertainty.

</details>


### [56] [Image-Space Refraction Correction for Underwater 3D Reconstruction: Warping Flat-Port Views into Pinhole Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07788)
*Chelim Lim,Tobias Fischer,Emilio Olivastri,Beverley Gorry,Michael Milford,Alejandro Fontan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于物理模型的图像空间折射校正方法，在重建前去除平口防水壳引入的水下折射畸变，可直接接入现有SfM与SLAM流程，消除重建形变并提升配准帧数与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消费级相机配平口防水壳成本低、易获取，广泛用于珊瑚礁与海底测绘，但平口界面的折射会导致重建场景与相机轨迹出现碗状形变，损害测绘与导航所需的度量精度。

Method: 在图像空间引入基于物理的折射校正，作为重建前的预处理；通过光线追踪仿真刻画折射畸变，并在两个场景结构不同的真实水下数据集上验证校正效果。方法与下游任务解耦，校正后图像可直接作为现有重建与SLAM算法的输入。

Result: 与常规SfM及折射感知SfM相比，本方法在消除重建形变的同时配准到更多帧，并保持较低的重投影误差；校正还能泛化到多种重建与视觉SLAM后端。

Conclusion: 该物理驱动、下游无关的折射校正可有效去除平口防水壳引起的畸变，具备广泛适用性，可无缝集成到各类水下视觉管线中。

Abstract: Consumer-grade cameras in flat-port housings are widely used for underwater exploration and mapping of coral reefs and seafloor habitats due to their low cost and accessibility. However, refraction at flat-port interfaces causes bowl-shaped deformation in reconstructed scenes and camera trajectories, compromising the metric accuracy required for mapping and navigation. To remove the dominant refractive distortion before reconstruction, we introduce a physics-based refraction correction in image space. Our method is downstream-agnostic: the refraction-corrected images can be directly used as input to existing reconstruction and SLAM algorithms. We characterize the refractive distortion through ray-tracing simulations and validate our correction on two real underwater datasets with differing scene structures. Compared with conventional and refractive Structure-from-Motion (SfM), our approach removes reconstruction deformation while registering more frames and maintaining low reprojection error. The correction further generalizes across diverse reconstruction and VSLAM backends, demonstrating its broad applicability to downstream vision pipelines.

</details>


### [57] [Geometry-Constrained Bidirectional Point Cloud Registration for Thin, Sheet-Like Heritage Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07793)
*Yuezhe Zhang,Lei Wei,Jingnan Du,Shuai Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对薄片状文物正反面点云配准中特征稀缺与旋转歧义问题，提出几何约束的双向配准方法，结合语义预处理、PCA归一化与厚度感知约束，通过旋转假设评估与点面ICP优化实现可靠配准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 薄片状文物易损，无法悬吊或粘贴标记，正反面须独立采集。两表面共享几何特征少、缺乏显式物理约束，导致配准出现旋转歧义、优化不稳定甚至结构塌陷，亟需一种无需接触且能保持结构完整性的配准方案。

Method: 方法包含三部分：语义引导的预处理、基于PCA的几何归一化、厚度感知的配准策略。将估计的物理厚度作为几何约束以维持结构完整性；通过评估有限个全局旋转假设解决旋转歧义，每个假设用点到平面ICP精化，并以与厚度尺度一致的几何感知适应度准则选取最优变换。

Result: 实验表明该方法在多数情况下性能相当或更优，尤其在投影面积一致性与物理合理的前后对齐方面表现突出。厚度感知约束与旋转假设评估降低了表面错误翻转却仍获得虚高数值评分的退化配置风险。

Conclusion: 该方法支持精细薄片状文物的可靠非接触数字化，缓解了配准中的旋转歧义与结构失稳问题。实现细节见 https://zyz-nwpu.github.io/GCBPCR/。

Abstract: Non-contact three-dimensional reconstruction of thin, sheet-like heritage artifacts poses significant geometric and registration challenges. Due to their fragility, these artifacts cannot be suspended or equipped with artificial markers, necessitating independent acquisition of their front and back surfaces. Subsequent registration proves difficult due to the limited number of shared geometric features and the scarcity of explicit physical constraints, which may result in rotational ambiguity, instability, and structural collapse during iterative optimization. To address these challenges, we propose a geometry-constrained bidirectional point cloud registration method specifically tailored for thin, sheet-like heritage artifacts. The method integrates semantic-guided preprocessing, Principal Component Analysis (PCA)-based geometric normalization, and a thickness-aware registration strategy. The estimated physical thickness is incorporated as a geometric constraint to preserve structural integrity during registration. Rotational ambiguity is resolved by evaluating a finite set of global rotation hypotheses, each refined using the point-to-plane Iterative Closest Point (ICP) algorithm, with the optimal transformation selected via a geometry-aware fitness criterion consistent with the thickness scale. Experimental results show that the proposed method achieves competitive or improved performance in most cases, particularly in projected area consistency and physically plausible front-back alignment. In addition, the thickness-aware constraint and rotation hypothesis evaluation reduce the risk of degenerate configurations in which the two surfaces are incorrectly flipped while still yielding deceptively acceptable numerical scores, supporting reliable non-contact digitization of delicate and thin heritage artifacts. Implementation details are available at https://zyz-nwpu.github.io/GCBPCR/.

</details>


### [58] [Efficient Gaussian Splatting Sequence Compression with Standard Video Codecs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07795)
*Qi Yang,Shuting Xia,Le Yang,Geert Van Der Auwera,Zhu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GSCV，一种基于视频编解码器的高斯泼溅序列压缩方法，通过Inter-PLAS提升帧间相关性并采用高比特深度图像管线，实现优于MPEG视频与点云基准的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视频的GS序列压缩依赖Parallel Linear Assignment Sorting (PLAS)及被跟踪的primitive信息将GS转为平滑2D视频；但大多数实际应用无法获取跟踪信息，此时原始PLAS因其随机性会导致帧间相关性弱的图像，压缩性能下降。

Method: GSCV引入简单高效的Inter-PLAS，使GS的I帧与P帧之间生成相近图像，从而大幅提升视频编解码器的帧间性能；并基于最先进视频编解码器构建新管线，采用高比特深度GS图像。

Result: 高比特深度图像管线带来更高可压缩性，同时提供更高的质量上界；实验显示GSCV在GS序列压缩上明显优于MPEG视频与点云类基准方法。代码已开源。

Conclusion: GSCV无需跟踪信息即可有效压缩GS序列，兼顾压缩率与重建质量，为GS序列压缩提供了实用且性能更优的方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel effective Gaussian Splatting (GS) sequence Compression method that utilizes the Video codec (GSCV). Existing video-based GS sequence compression relies on the Parallel Linear Assignment Sorting (PLAS) and tracked primitive information to convert GS into smooth 2D videos. However, tracked information is not available for most practical applications, and without it, using the vanilla PLAS can generate images exhibiting weak inter-frame correlation, due to its stochastic nature. GSCV incorporates a simple yet efficient Inter-PLAS method to produce close images between the I- and P-frames of GS, enhancing the inter-frame performance of video codec greatly. GSCV also realizes a new pipeline based on the state-of-the-art video codecs with high bit-depth GS images, achieving higher compressibility while simultaneously providing a higher quality upper bound. Experimental results show that the proposed GSCV exhibits obviously improved performance over MPEG video and point cloud-based anchors in GS sequence compression. The code is available at https://github.com/Qi-Yangsjtu/GSCV.

</details>


### [59] [Towards benchmarking Western Bluebird detection in the wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07802)
*Estela Monserrat Arriaga Santana,Julian Rosas Scull,Ibeth P. Alarcón,Bibiana Montoya,Aylin Sosa Mejía,Hugo Jair Escalante*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发布了一个西蓝鸲检测与分割基准数据集（6000+标注图像、41段录像、4K、小目标占比小），并系统评测了监督检测器、开放词汇模型与分割方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然环境中鸟类监测困难：目标相对场景小、背景杂乱、光照变化、视角多变；同时缺乏大规模真实数据集，难以支撑行为模式研究。

Method: 构建包含6000余张标注图像、来自41个录像会话的高分辨率（4K）野外数据集的西蓝鸲检测与分割基准；评测监督检测器、开放词汇模型（零样本与微调）以及分割方法。

Result: 监督检测器整体最可靠：Faster R-CNN取得最高检测mAP，RT-DETR在精度-召回权衡上最佳；开放词汇模型零样本表现差，但微调后显著提升，YOLO-World可媲美监督方法并取得最高精度、F1与mAP@0.5；分割上监督方法显著优于Grounded-SAM和SAM 3，Mask R-CNN取得最高mask mAP，YOLOv8-Seg精度最高且推理最快；失败原因不仅取决于目标尺寸，还受表观尺度、亮度、对比度、杂乱度、模糊、拥挤和录像会话差异影响。

Conclusion: 在杂乱生态场景中零样本鸟类检测难度大，小目标设定下领域自适应至关重要。

Abstract: Bird monitoring in natural environments is challenging due to the small size of some species of birds relative to the scene, background clutter, variability in illumination, and the observers' viewpoint. Progress is further limited by the scarcity of large-scale, realistic datasets, which are essential for understanding behavioral patterns. To address this gap, we introduce a new benchmark dataset for the detection and segmentation of Western bluebirds (Sialia Mexicana), comprising over 6,000 labeled images from 41 recording sessions. The dataset features high-resolution (4K) in-the-wild images in which birds occupy only a small fraction of the image. We evaluated supervised detectors, open-vocabulary models under zero-shot and fine-tuned settings, and segmentation approaches. Supervised detectors remain the most reliable overall, with Faster R-CNN achieving the highest detection mAP and RT-DETR offering the best precision-recall trade-off. Open-vocabulary models perform poorly in zero-shot settings; however, fine-tuning substantially improves their performance, with YOLO-World becoming competitive with supervised methods and achieving the highest precision, F1-score, and mAP@0.5. For segmentation, supervised methods significantly outperform Grounded-SAM and SAM 3: Mask R-CNN achieves the highest mask mAP, while YOLOv8-Seg provides the best precision and fastest inference. A diagnostic analysis further shows that failures are not explained by object size alone, but by a combination of apparent scale, brightness, contrast, clutter, blur, crowding, and recording-session variation. Overall, our findings highlight the difficulty of zero-shot bird detection in cluttered ecological scenes and underscore the importance of domain adaptation in small-object settings.

</details>


### [60] [CHARTER: Auditing Reference Substitution in Hierarchical Compact-Evidence Evaluation for Computational Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07843)
*Hyun Do Jung,Jungwon Choi,Soojung Choi,Yujin Oh,Hwiyoung Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 CHARTER，一套参考感知的评估章程，用于显式声明意图目标与评估参考、量化候选过滤引起的预测偏移，并审计比较结论的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数字病理的全切片图像多示例学习（MIL）中，常用紧凑证据解释或审计模型预测。层次化紧凑证据流程里的候选过滤会产生"候选条件预测"，与原始全袋预测并存。若评估参考发生变化而意图目标仍是原始全袋预测，则同一紧凑证据的保真度度量会改变，候选策略之间的比较结论也可能改变。这一隐性依赖此前缺乏显式规范。

Method: 提出 CHARTER（参考感知评估章程），要求研究者三步操作：DECLARE（声明意图目标与评估参考）、QUANTIFY（量化候选过滤导致的预测偏移）、AUDIT（审计比较结论的稳定性）。在五个随机种子的 Random-K 审计中进行 15 组比较，并设计匹配的原生排序压力测试。

Result: 15 组比较中有 4 组出现确定的结论反转；在匹配的原生排序压力测试中，ACMIL 比较从 REVERSED（反转）变为 PRESERVED（保持）。

Conclusion: CHARTER 将隐含的候选过滤与参考选择转变为可审计的评估规范，有助于区分"意图预测的真实保持"与"因改变被解释预测而产生的表观增益"。

Abstract: In digital pathology, compact evidence is often used to explain or audit predictions made by whole-slide image multiple instance learning models. In hierarchical compact-evidence pipelines, candidate filtering introduces a strategy-specific candidate-conditioned prediction alongside the original full-bag prediction. If the evaluation reference changes while the intended target remains the original full-bag prediction, however, not only can the measured fidelity of the same compact evidence change, but comparisons between competing candidate strategies can also change. To make this dependence explicit, we introduce CHARTER, a reference-aware evaluation charter that asks researchers to DECLARE the intended target and reference, QUANTIFY candidate-induced prediction shift, and AUDIT the stability of comparative conclusions. Across the 15 comparisons in our main five-seed Random-K audit, 4 showed determinate reversals; in a matched native-ranking stress test, the ACMIL comparison changed from REVERSED to PRESERVED. CHARTER turns otherwise implicit candidate-filtering and reference choices into an auditable evaluation specification, helping distinguish genuine preservation of the intended prediction from apparent gains induced by changing the prediction being explained.

</details>


### [61] [CueRator: Agentic Search for Symbolic Rules to Adapt Frozen Multimodal Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07868)
*Sunchan Park,Beomkwon Cho,Kyeongbo Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CueRator 是一个面向策略感知决策规则发现的智能体框架，通过搜索将冻结的对比多模态编码器的跨模态相似度转换为预测的决策规则，在开放词汇音视频事件感知上兼顾自适应性与泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在自适应性与对未见类别的泛化性之间存在权衡：训练模块虽能自适应但牺牲泛化，固定规则则反之。

Method: 该框架将可泛化的符号化决策规则与一个轻量级策略结合，后者按视频预测规则参数以实现自适应；并采用报告引导的多智能体循环离线发现规则，评估每个候选的表达能力上限以及训练策略能否实现该规则。

Result: 在 OV-AVEBench 上，CueRator 将总平均分从 57.8 提升至 60.2，未见类别性能从 55.8 提升至 59.9，并将已见-未见差距从 7.1 缩小至 1.2。消融实验表明增益来自规则公式与策略两方面，且两种反馈信号对有效搜索均属必要。该方法在另外两个音视频事件感知任务上也优于各自基线，且所发现的规则在更换编码器后仅需重训策略仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: CueRator 通过符号化规则与轻量策略的结合，有效缓解了开放词汇音视频事件感知中的自适应-泛化权衡，代码已开源。

Abstract: Large language model agents have been used to search over symbolic structures such as programs and equations. We propose CueRator, an agentic framework for policy-aware decision-rule discovery, which adapts frozen contrastive multimodal encoders by searching for the decision rule that converts their cross-modal similarities into predictions. We validate it on open-vocabulary audio-visual event perception, where existing methods involve a trade-off between adaptivity and generalization to unseen categories: trained modules adapt at the cost of generalization, and fixed rules the reverse. The framework pairs a symbolic formulation for generalization with a lightweight policy that predicts its parameters per video for adaptivity. A report-guided multi-agent loop discovers the formulation offline, evaluating each candidate on its expressive ceiling and on whether a trained policy can realize it. On OV-AVEBench, CueRator raises the total average from 57.8 to 60.2 and unseen-category performance from 55.8 to 59.9 over the best existing method, reducing the seen-unseen gap from 7.1 to 1.2. Ablations attribute the gains to both the formulation and the policy and show that both feedback signals are necessary for effective search. CueRator also improves over the respective baselines on two further audio-visual event perception tasks, and the discovered rule remains competitive across encoders with only the policy retrained. Code is available at https://github.com/cvsp-lab/cuerator.

</details>


### [62] [Revar3r: gauge-aware perturbation uncertainty for feed-forward 3d reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07883)
*Sammam Mahdi,Fariha Binta Salim,Rakin Bin Rabbani,Aniqua Nusrat Zereen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究针对三维重建点图的不确定性估计，提出ReVar3R：在冻结模型上先把多次预测对齐到同一相似变换框架，再计算逐点方差，从而消除因输出旋转等未观测对称性带来的“伪不确定性”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对远距离点，即使输入等价、模型冻结，输出帧也会发生微小旋转，使训练免扰动类不确定性把对称性变化误当作误差。内置置信度多数条件下表现不佳，而训练式证据头需模型专用监督，且点图存在随场景尺度增长的闭式方差项，可能淹没真正误差信号。

Method: 推导与误差无关、随场景范围增长的闭式方差项并用模拟复现，在30组真实VGGT视图集上验证其‖x_p‖²特征；ReVar3R在计算逐点方差前稳健注册预测到公共相似框架，不重训、不改动冻结模型；可选校准与融合使用留出划分。

Result: 在VGGT、π3、MASt3R三个骨干与六个数据集上，同一估计器在18个条件中的15个把AUSE降至内置置信度以下；分阶段评估中免标签核心11/18胜出、等权融合12/18、留出权重14/18、纳入内置信号15/18。对比训练式证据头：后者幅值校准更好且在其训练域领先，ReVar3R可跨骨干迁移。

Conclusion: ReVar3R无需重训即可缓解输出对称性导致的伪不确定性，改善点过滤排序；但不能检测稳定的系统性偏差、辅助新视图合成或跨域迁移校准。

Abstract: A correctly reconstructed distant point appears uncertain even when a frozen 3D model processes equivalent inputs because its output frame rotates fractionally. This exposes a weakness of trainingfree perturbation uncertainty: when outputs contain an unobserved symmetry, run-to-run variation potentially reflects symmetry rather than error. Existing alternatives have trade-offs: built-in confidence is outperformed in most evaluated conditions, while trained evidential heads require modelspecific supervision. For point maps, this research derives a closed-form, error-independent variance term that grows with scene extent and potentially overwhelms the desired signal. Simulation reproduces the effect; all 30 real VGGT view-sets tested exhibit its predicted $\|x_p\|^2$ signature. ReVar3R robustly registers predictions to a common similarity frame before computing per-point variance, without retraining or modifying the frozen model. Optional calibration and fusion use a held-out split. Across VGGT, π3, and MASt3R on six datasets, the same estimator on every backbone lowers AUSE below built-in confidence in 15 of 18 conditions. The staged evaluation yields 11 of 18 wins for the label-free core, 12/18 for label-free equal-weight fusion, 14/18 with held-out weights, and 15/18 when the built-in signal is included. Against a trained evidential head, the result is a trade-off: the head calibrates magnitude better and leads in its training domain, whereas ReVar3R transfers across backbones without adaptation. Its ranking improves point filtering, but it does not detect stable systematic bias, aid novel-view synthesis, or transfer calibration across domains.

</details>


### [63] [Unsupervised Long-Tailed Adaptation of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07903)
*Keliang Chen,Yaxin Hou,Hui Liu,Yuheng Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出无监督长尾适配(ULTA)新场景,揭示现有方法在长尾分布下头类性能骤降的反常现象,并提出MARS模型(边界保持对齐+边际感知自精炼)加以解决,在9个基准上平均精度提升4.71个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型的下游适配方法普遍假设无标注数据服从均匀分布,从而伪标签分布也是均匀的;但真实数据常为长尾分布。作者因此形式化ULTA场景,并观察到与监督长尾学习(尾部类受损最重)相反的现象:头类性能急剧下降。进一步分析发现,分布失配不仅侵蚀头类决策边界,还把头类样本推入易混淆类别,加剧模型固有偏置。

Method: 提出MARS(Margin-Aware Refinement with Structural alignment)模型。其一是Boundary-Preserving Alignment:以零样本VLM作为固定视觉参照,抑制训练目标中缺乏视觉支持的概率上升,从而缓解头类边界侵蚀。其二是Margin-aware Self-Refinement:采用动态调整策略精炼尾部类与易混淆类,同时防止预测偏置。

Result: 在九个基准数据集上的大量实验表明,MARS优于当前最先进方法,平均精度提升4.71个百分点。

Conclusion: 作者定义了无监督长尾适配这一新场景,揭示分布失配导致头类边界侵蚀与样本误入混淆类的机理,并通过边界保持对齐与边际感知自精炼两个组件有效缓解该问题,验证了MARS的有效性与通用性。

Abstract: Adapting vision-language models to downstream tasks has achieved remarkable success by leveraging pseudo-labels generated from unlabeled data. Existing methods typically assume a uniform unlabeled data distribution, and thus the resulting pseudo-label distribution is likewise uniform. However, real-world data distributions are often long-tailed. To tackle this, we formalize a new scenario termed Unsupervised Long-Tailed Adaptation (ULTA). Under this scenario, existing methods exhibit a contrasting phenomenon: head-class performance drops sharply, which is distinct from supervised long-tailed learning where tail classes suffer the most. In particular, we uncover that the distributional mismatch not only erodes head-class boundaries, but also pushes head samples into confusable classes, reinforcing the model's inherent bias. To address these issues, we propose a novel model called Margin-Aware Refinement with Structural alignment (MARS). Specifically, we mitigate head-class boundary erosion via Boundary-Preserving Alignment, which takes the zero-shot VLM as a fixed visual reference to suppress probability increases that lack visual support in the training targets. Building upon this, we introduce Margin-aware Self-Refinement, which employs a dynamic adjustment strategy to refine tail and confusable classes while preventing prediction bias. Extensive experiments on nine benchmark datasets demonstrate that MARS outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving an average accuracy improvement of 4.71 percentage points.

</details>


### [64] [Diverse Motion Customization via Control-based Dynamic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07911)
*Youngyoon Choi,Kihyun Kim,Jeongwoo Shin,Joonseok Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于控制的运动定制框架CMC，将定制学习形式化为随机最优控制问题，从结构上避免生成过程向参考视频塌缩，从而缓解内容泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成中的运动定制存在内容泄漏问题：参考视频的外观属性会无意间传播到生成结果中。作者将其归因于学习目标被直接建模为对参考视频的回归，导致生成过程向参考视频塌缩。

Method: 提出Control-based Motion Customization (CMC)训练框架。核心是用随机最优控制(SOC)形式化定制过程，引导生成动力学趋向目标运动，同时避免向参考视频塌缩。在此框架下，定制视频获得目标运动但仍位于预训练模型的提示条件分布内，外观由文本提示而非参考视频决定。为提升效率，对SOC形式进行裁剪：去除显式奖励项，并引入仅关注早期生成阶段的时间步自适应运动代价。

Result: 时间步自适应运动代价使训练加速2.5倍。大量实验表明CMC能有效缓解内容泄漏，在多种场景下实现有竞争力的运动保真度，同时保持基础模型的多样性。

Conclusion: 将运动定制重构为随机最优控制问题可从根本上抑制内容泄漏，兼顾运动保真度与生成多样性，并通过简化SOC形式显著提升训练效率。

Abstract: Despite recent advances in video generation, motion customization remains challenging due to content leakage, where appearance attributes from the reference video unintentionally propagate into the generated output. We identify this issue as a consequence of the generative process collapsing toward the reference video, which arises from formulating the learning objective as a direct regression on the reference. To address this, we propose Control-based Motion Customization (CMC), a principled training framework that is structurally robust to content leakage. Our key idea is to steer generative dynamics toward desired motion while avoiding collapse toward the reference video, which we formalize using Stochastic Optimal Control (SOC). Under this formulation, customized videos acquire the target motion yet remain within the pre-trained model's prompt-conditional distribution, where appearance is determined by the text prompt rather than the reference video. Furthermore, to improve efficiency, we tailor the SOC formulation to motion customization by eliminating the need for an explicit reward and introducing a timestep-adaptive motion cost that focuses only on early generative stages, accelerating training by 2.5 times. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CMC effectively mitigates content leakage and achieves competitive motion fidelity while preserving the diversity of the base model across diverse scenarios.

</details>


### [65] [Multimodal Knowledge Distillation for Gastric Adenocarcinoma Classification from Whole-Slide Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07913)
*Shrihari Dumbre,Bikash Santra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多模态知识蒸馏（MKD）框架：教师模型用低秩多模态融合（LMF）结合WSI图像编码器与临床文本编码器，学生模型蒸馏知识实现仅图像推理；在PatchGastric上平均准确率较SOTA提升≥3.35%，且不依赖transformer融合、多任务学习或大语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胃腺癌（GA）是全球癌症相关死亡的主要原因，全切片图像（WSI）的组织病理学亚型准确分类对治疗决策至关重要。现有多模态方法虽能结合病理报告文本与WSI提升分类性能，但通常依赖计算开销大的transformer架构和大语言模型，效率较低。

Method: 多模态知识蒸馏（MKD）框架：将每张WSI表示为补丁包（bag of patches）并配以切片级诊断描述文本；图像端使用预训练WSI图像编码器，文本端使用临床文本编码器，通过低秩多模态融合（LMF）在训练阶段高效建模跨模态交互。教师模型学习融合的图像-文本表示用于亚型分类，学生模型蒸馏该知识，从而在推理阶段仅需图像输入即可准确分类。

Result: 在PatchGastric基准数据集上评估，平均准确率较当前最先进方法至少高出3.35%；且方法未使用基于transformer的融合、多任务学习或大语言模型。源代码已开源（https://github.com/helomelo1/MKD-LMF）。

Conclusion: MKD结合LMF可在不依赖高开销transformer融合与大语言模型的前提下，有效利用病理文本提升胃腺癌组织学亚型分类性能，并支持训练时多模态、推理时仅图像的实用部署范式。

Abstract: Gastric adenocarcinoma (GA) is a leading cause of cancer-related mortality worldwide, and accurate histopathological subtype classification from whole-slide images (WSIs) is essential for effective treatment planning. While multimodal approaches that integrate pathology report text with WSIs can improve classification, existing methods often depend on computationally expensive transformer architectures and large language models. We propose a multimodal knowledge distillation (MKD) framework that combines a pretrained WSI image encoder and a clinical text encoder using Low-Rank Multimodal Fusion (LMF) to efficiently model cross-modal interactions during training. Each WSI is represented as a bag of patches paired with a slide-level diagnostic caption. The teacher model learns fused image-text representations for subtype classification, while the student model distills this knowledge to enable accurate image-only inference. We evaluate our method on the PatchGastric benchmark dataset and achieve at least 3.35% higher mean accuracy than state-of-the-art approaches, without relying on transformer-based fusion, multi-task learning, or large language models. The source code is available at https://github.com/helomelo1/MKD-LMF.

</details>


### [66] [Can We Model the Artifacts Explicitly? Disentangle Artifacts via Pairwise Edit Relations for Image Manipulation Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07916)
*Xuekang Zhu,Kaiwen Feng,Ruifeng Wang,Xiwen Wang,Xiaochen Ma,Bo Du,Changjiang Jiang,Chenfan Qu,Songyu Ye,Xia Du,Wentao Feng,Jian Liu,Ji-Zhe Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文将图像篡改定位(IML)重新解释为隐变量问题,提出显式建模伪影的两阶段学习范式PAL,并构建EditGroup-45K数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IML方法普遍建模为全监督学习任务,直接拟合P(y|x)。作者指出其不足的根源在于对伪影采用隐式建模策略,需要显式建模伪影z。

Method: 将IML重述为P(y|x)=∫P(y|z)P(z|x)dz的隐变量问题。由于缺少伪影直接标签,采用特征解耦实现显式建模,提出两阶段范式:PAL阶段基于编辑关系估计P(z|x),SL阶段估计P(y|z)。并构建源锚定、按编辑组组织的EditGroup-45K数据集支持成对学习。

Result: 大量实验表明PAL范式在多种IML架构上取得一致性提升;实证分析验证PAL通过特征解耦确实显式捕获了伪影。

Conclusion: 显式建模伪影是提升IML性能的有效途径,PAL范式具备良好的架构通用性,代码与数据集已开源。

Abstract: Image Manipulation Localization (IML) is commonly formulated as a fully supervised learning task that estimates the optimal manipulation mask $y$ for a given image $x$. In this work, we first reveal the latent nature of artifacts and thus reinterpret IML as a latent-variable problem, $P(y|x)=\int P(y|z)\,P(z|x)\,dz$, where $z$ denotes the artifacts. Following this interpretation, we pinpoint the cause for the current IML models' insufficiency as their implicit artifacts modeling strategy, highlighting the necessity of modeling $z$ in an explicit manner. Without direct labels, feature disentanglement is the most appropriate solution for this explicit modeling. Accordingly, we propose a two-stage learning paradigm with the Pairwise Artifacts Learning (PAL) and Standard Localization (SL) phases to estimate $P(z|x)$ and $P(y|z)$ via edit relations. To support our edit-relation-based learning, we further curate EditGroup-45K, a source-anchored dataset organized into edit groups for pair construction. Extensive experiments show that our PAL paradigm yields consistent improvements across diverse IML architectures, and empirical analyses further verify that PAL does capture artifacts explicitly through feature disentanglement. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/venus-guangjian/PAL

</details>


### [67] [TF-PRVR: Training-Free Partially Relevant Video Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07925)
*Giyeol Kim,Chanho Eom*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TF-PRVR，首个用于部分相关视频检索（PRVR）的免训练框架，利用冻结的视觉-语言特征构建视频特定的层次化表示，通过频率多尺度分析生成自适应时序边界，并在多尺度图上传播查询相关性进行时刻感知打分，避免源域过拟合，在多数据集上取得一致性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PRVR方法存在两大局限：一是固定的视频分解方案导致语义稀释；二是任务特定训练引发源域过拟合，损害预训练视觉-语言模型的通用对齐能力。

Method: TF-PRVR为免训练框架：1）基于冻结的视觉-语言特征构建视频特定的层次化表示；2）从帧级特征导出时序语义信号，采用基于频率的多尺度分析识别自适应时序边界，生成具有连贯事件级语义的层次化片段；3）在这些片段上构建统一多尺度图，在时序与语义相关的节点间传播查询相关性；4）采用时刻感知打分策略，跨尺度聚合时序对齐的相关性，强化一致支持的片段并抑制孤立误响应。

Result: 在具有多样视觉与时序特性的多个数据集上实验显示，TF-PRVR性能表现一致，验证了免训练PRVR方向的可行性。

Conclusion: TF-PRVR作为首个免训练PRVR框架，保留预训练视觉-语言模型的通用对齐能力，避免数据集特定过拟合，为PRVR提供了一条实用的免训练方向。

Abstract: Partially Relevant Video Retrieval (PRVR) aims to retrieve untrimmed videos containing moments relevant to a given text query. Despite recent progress, existing PRVR methods suffer from two key limitations: a fixed video decomposition scheme that causes semantic dilution, and source-domain overfitting induced by task-specific training. In this paper, we propose TF-PRVR, the first training-free framework for PRVR. TF-PRVR leverages frozen vision-language features to construct video-specific hierarchical representations.
